- **triage**: Logical-judgment front door of the bug→PR pipeline. Hands each epic to `/assess --epic N`. Optionally (`--feedback`) ends by walking the operator's pending-feedback queue — the issues parked on *them* — one at a time so they can… ([Towheads/temperloop_legacy](https://github.com/Towheads/temperloop_legacy/blob/HEAD/claude/commands/triage.md))
